Personal care

Your personal care routine, built around when you actually wake up and sleep

For oily skin that gets the occasional pimple, on a night-shift schedule. Prices and availability were checked on 26–29 September 2026 at Watsons Philippines and Watsons’ Lazada store; they change often, so treat them as a guide.

A typical day for you

The times are only an example. Follow your body, not the clock.

How to read this routine

  • Wake-up routine is the first thing you do after getting up, usually in the afternoon.
  • Before-sleep routine is the last thing you do before bed, whether that is 2 am, 4 am or 9 am.
  • Sunscreen depends on daylight, not the clock. Wear it after waking if you will see daylight before you sleep; light through the window at your desk counts. Skip it if you wake after sunset and stay under lamps until bed. If the sun rises while you are still working by a window, apply it then. You will wash it off before bed anyway.
  • Pick one product per step. Your current product is listed first; the alternatives below it are things you could switch to, not extra layers to add.
  • Niacinamide appears in your cleanser, serum and retinol cream. At these amounts that is fine and common: about 5% from the serum, only a trace in the retinol cream, and the cleanser rinses off; your moisturizer has none. Only be careful if you swap to a 10% serum or add a niacinamide moisturizer; stop if you get stinging or flushing.
  • Introduce one new product at a time and give it two weeks before judging it.
  • Moisturizer in the daytime is optional for you. The cleanser-then-sunscreen routines you see in videos are a valid minimalist approach for oily skin: dermatologists say you can skip the morning moisturizer if your sunscreen feels hydrating enough. Where they all agree is that moisturizer matters at bedtime, and that sunscreen is the non-negotiable daytime step. The wake-up routine below marks the moisturizer as optional and tells you when to keep it.
  • Budget picks. Each step now also lists one alternative at ₱500 or under per brand origin (plus a Filipino one for the cleanser). They are marked in the cards; a few are only under ₱500 in a small size or on Watsons’ promo price, and the card says so.
  • Retinol is optional, and only before sleep. It helps keep pores clear and skin smooth, but it is the strongest product in this plan: start on 2 nights a week, never on the same night as a leave-on acid or vitamin C product, and keep up the sunscreen on daylight wake-ups. You keep your Quick FX acid wash on retinol nights, so watch for stinging; the before-sleep routine shows how to use both.
  • Toner is optional. It is a watery layer pressed on after cleansing that adds hydration and soothing ingredients before the serum; skipping it loses nothing essential. An acid toner (AHA, BHA or PHA) doubles the acids in your Quick FX wash: once a day at most, and never on retinol nights.

Click any product photo to see it full size.

Wake-up routine

Do this within an hour of getting up, whatever time that is.

1

Cleanse

How much
Pea- to nickel-sized. For Perfect Whip, about 2 cm.
How to apply
Wet your face with lukewarm water, never hot. Lather gel or foam types between your palms first, then massage for a full 60 seconds, spending extra time on the forehead, nose and chin where oil pools while you sleep. Rinse well, then pat with a clean towel until your skin is damp, not fully dry.
Then wait
No wait. Go straight to the toner, or to the serum if you skip it, while your skin is still slightly damp.
Choose one of these 8. Do not use them together.
Your current product₱500 and under
Quick FX Pimple Eraser Facial Gel Cleanser
Sizes, prices and how long each lasts
SizePriceLasts about
150 ml₱329about 2½ months
75 ml₱199about 5 weeks
Where to buy
Watsons (in store and online); Quick FX official Shopee and Lazada stores
Key actives
Salicylic acid (BHA)Glycolic acid (AHA)NiacinamideTea tree oilAloeAllantoinEthyl ascorbic acid (vitamin C)

Why it fits you. Your wash for both routines, from a Filipino brand: salicylic acid (BHA) keeps pores clear for the odd pimple, a little glycolic acid (AHA) smooths, and niacinamide, tea tree and aloe calm, in a sulfate-free gel that rinses clean on oily skin. The 75 ml has 191 Watsons ratings; the 150 ml is ₱329.

Watch out. Two exfoliating acids, twice a day, and you keep it on retinol nights too. Warning: acid and retinol on the same night raise the risk of stinging, redness and peeling. On those nights, lather it for 20–30 seconds instead of 60, rinse well and let your face dry completely before the retinol; if your skin stings or peels, skip the retinol that night. Its label warns that the glycolic acid (an AHA) raises sun sensitivity, so wear sunscreen on every daylight wake-up. It is fragrance-free, though tea tree oil has a scent of its own.

2

Toner (optional)

How much
About 1 ml, a coin-sized pool in your palm.
How to apply
Right after cleansing, while your face is still slightly damp. Pour it into your palm and press it onto the forehead, cheeks, nose and chin with flat hands until it sinks in, or soak a cotton pad and sweep it outward from the centre of your face without rubbing. The Japanese “lotions” here (Hada Labo, Naturie) are toners made for this patting; Naturie suggests a second or third layer when skin feels dry. An acid toner (AHA, BHA or PHA) doubles the acids in your Quick FX wash, so use one at most once a day.
Then wait
30–60 seconds, until your skin no longer feels wet, then the serum.
Skip it when
you are short on time, or your skin stings or feels raw; it is the one step here you can drop without losing anything essential. Skip an acid toner on retinol nights.
Choose one of these 7. Do not use them together.

3

Serum

How much
2–3 drops, about half a dropper, or the same 2–3 drops from a tube.
How to apply
Drop it into your palm, then press onto damp skin with flat fingers: forehead, both cheeks, chin. Press, do not rub.
Then wait
30–60 seconds, until it no longer feels wet.
Choose one of these 7. Do not use them together.

4

Moisturize (optional for oily skin)

How much
Pea- to blueberry-sized.
How to apply
Warm it between two fingertips, press onto the cheeks first and smooth outward and upward, then finish the forehead, nose and chin with whatever is left. Oily skin wants a thin, even layer, not a thick one.
Then wait
2–3 minutes before sunscreen, so the sunscreen can form an even film on top.
Skip it when
your sunscreen already feels hydrating and your skin is not tight after cleansing. Dermatologists agree oily skin can go cleanser → serum → sunscreen in the daytime; the moisturizer becomes essential at bedtime instead. Keep it on wake-ups with no sunscreen, or if your skin ever feels tight or flaky.
Choose one of these 8. Do not use them together.

5

Sunscreen, only if you will see daylight

How much
Two full finger-lengths, about a quarter teaspoon, for the face and neck.
How to apply
Dot it on the forehead, both cheeks, nose and chin, then spread with flat fingers until even. Lay it down rather than rubbing it in like a lotion; rubbing thins the protective film. Include the ears and the back of the neck if you are going outside.
Then wait
10–15 minutes before going out or sitting in direct window light. Reapply every 2 hours in direct sun or after sweating; indoors by a window, one application per waking period is enough.
Skip it when
you will have no daylight at all before you sleep.
Choose one of these 8. Do not use them together.

Before-sleep routine

Do this right before you actually lie down, whether that is 2 am, 4 am or 9 am. The point is to take the day’s sunscreen, sweat and oil off before you sleep on it. If you fall asleep at the laptop, cleanse the moment you wake instead.

1

Cleanse

Same product you chose for the wake-up routine. One gentle cleanse is enough to remove these gel sunscreens. If you used a lot of sunscreen and still feel a film, cleanse a second time with the same product instead of buying a separate oil cleanser.

2

Toner (optional)

Same product you chose for the wake-up routine. Same amount, pressed on after cleansing. On retinol nights use it only if it is acid-free, or skip it, so nothing adds to the Quick FX acids under the retinol.

3

Serum

Same product you chose for the wake-up routine. Same 2–3 drops pressed onto damp skin. Wait 30–60 seconds.

4

Retinol, twice a week to start (optional)

How much
One pea-sized amount for the whole face; more does not work faster, it only irritates.
How to apply
On retinol nights, skip leave-on acids and vitamin C. You keep your Quick FX wash, but it carries acids, and acid plus retinol on one night raises the risk of stinging and peeling: lather it for only 20–30 seconds, rinse well, then let your face dry completely before the retinol (the American Academy of Dermatology suggests 20–30 minutes after washing); damp skin stings more. A niacinamide serum can go on in between. Dot the retinol on the forehead, cheeks and chin and spread it in a thin layer, away from the eyes, the corners of the nose and the lips. Use it 2 nights a week for the first month, then every other night if your skin stays calm.
Then wait
Close the lid right away, then moisturize after a few minutes. Some dryness, flaking or redness in the first weeks is normal while skin adjusts; if it stings or peels, use it less often, or put the moisturizer on first and the retinol over it. Judge it after 2–4 months for pimples; fine lines take longer. Retinol breaks down in light, so if you sleep while the sun is up, draw the curtains, and wear sunscreen on every daylight wake-up.
Skip it when
your skin is sunburned, peeling, stinging or freshly irritated; you had a peel or used a leave-on AHA or BHA product that night (your rinse-off Quick FX wash is the exception you chose); or you are pregnant, breastfeeding or trying to conceive.
Choose one of these 6. Do not use them together.

5

Moisturize

Same product you chose for the wake-up routine. At bedtime you can go slightly more generous, blueberry-sized, because nothing goes on top. If you wake up greasy, go back to a pea.

6

Lip treatment, the last thing before sleep

How much
A thick layer, more than you would wear during the day.
How to apply
Wait a minute after your moisturizer so it is not sliding around, then apply with a clean fingertip, or the spatula for the tub types, from the centre of the lips outward. Do not rub it in and do not lick it off; go to bed. Wipe any residue off with a tissue when you wake. If your lips are flaky, a soft damp towel rubbed gently once a week is enough; skip sugar scrubs.
Then wait
Nothing else goes on after this. Lights out.
Choose one of these 7. Do not use them together.

If a pimple shows up

Do not squeeze it. After the moisturizer step, a hydrocolloid pimple patch (for example Acnes patches, ₱230 at Watsons) is the one extra you can safely add. Take it off when you wake.

Every active ingredient, explained

All the actives and notable ingredients across the 57 products above, in plain language, with the products that contain each one.

Hydrators and barrier builders

Hyaluronic acid (sodium hyaluronate)

A sugar molecule that holds many times its weight in water. It pulls moisture into the top layer of skin so it feels plump rather than tight. Works best applied to damp skin and sealed with a moisturizer.

Found inDermoRepubliqNeutrogenaSKIN1004Hada Labo (Rohto)Bioré (Kao)MedihealHada Labo (Rohto)Skin Aqua (Sunplay, Rohto)Luxe OrganixCeraVeCOSRXAnuaHada Labo (Rohto)Smoochkins
Glycerin

The most reliable humectant in skincare; your skin makes it too. It draws water in and keeps it there, which is why nearly every product here uses it.

Found inSenka (Shiseido)La Roche-PosayBeauty of JoseonBioré (Kao)NeutrogenaSKIN1004Hada Labo (Rohto)VaselineQuick FXSimpleNaturie (Imju)Some By MiLuxe OrganixOlayCOSRXFranklyCetaphilAnuaHada Labo (Rohto)Luxe OrganixNiveaRound LabNaturie (Imju)SmoochkinsVaseline
Ceramides (NP, AP, EOP, NS)

Fats your skin uses like mortar between bricks. Topping them up seals the barrier, so skin loses less water and gets irritated less, which matters when you cleanse twice a day.

Found inSKIN1004CeraVeFranklySmoochkins
Cholesterol and phytosphingosine

The two partners of ceramides in the skin barrier. Formulas that include all three repair the barrier better than ceramides alone.

Found inSKIN1004CeraVeSmoochkins
Trehalose

A plant sugar that protects cells from drying out; a gentle extra humectant.

Found inSKIN1004Hada Labo (Rohto)
Beta-glucan

A sugar from oats or yeast that hydrates and calms at the same time; often used to soothe skin after acne treatments.

Found inSKIN1004
Panthenol (pro-vitamin B5)

Converts to vitamin B5 in the skin. Softens, hydrates and speeds up the recovery of irritated skin and chapped lips.

Found inMedihealVaselineSimpleLuxe OrganixCOSRXAnuaRound Lab
Snail secretion filtrate

A hydrating, soothing extract rich in glycoproteins and a little hyaluronic acid; a K-beauty staple for calming skin.

Found inDermoRepubliq
Ectoin

A small protective molecule that some bacteria make to survive heat, salt and drying out. On skin it holds water and helps the barrier recover, and it rarely irritates; it is a key active of the Smoochkins gel cream.

Found inSmoochkins

Oil, pores and pimple helpers

Niacinamide (vitamin B3)

The workhorse of your routine. It regulates oil production, calms redness, strengthens the barrier and slowly evens tone. 2–5% is plenty; 10% adds little for most people and raises the chance of stinging.

Found inDermoRepubliqHada Labo (Rohto)Beauty of JoseonQuick FXGarnierLuxe OrganixCeraVeOlayFranklyCetaphilLuxe OrganixVaseline
Zinc (zinc PCA, zinc gluconate)

A mineral salt that helps reduce shine and has a mild antibacterial effect.

Found inLa Roche-PosayGarnier
BHA: salicylic acid, capryloyl salicylic acid and betaine salicylate

Oil-soluble exfoliants that get inside pores and loosen the plugs that become blackheads and pimples. Salicylic acid (Quick FX, Some By Mi, Garnier) is the classic; betaine salicylate (COSRX, Anua) is a softer cousin. Low levels in a cleanser are safe daily; two acid products on the same day is usually too much.

Found inQuick FXSome By MiGarnier
Tea tree leaf oil

A plant oil with proven activity against acne bacteria, found in the Quick FX cleanser. Good for pimple-prone skin, though a few people are sensitive to it.

Found inQuick FXSome By Mi
O-Cymen-5-OL (isopropyl methylphenol)

An antibacterial common in Japanese medicated skincare that targets the bacteria involved in pimples; the reason Melano CC is marketed for acne marks and prevention.

Found inMelano CC (Rohto)
Silica (Airlicium) and starches

Porous particles that soak up sebum so skin stays matte for hours.

Found inLa Roche-Posay
Glycolic acid (AHA) and gluconolactone (PHA)

Water-soluble exfoliants that loosen dead surface cells so skin looks smoother. Glycolic acid is the strongest and can raise sun sensitivity; PHA is the gentlest. Rinse-off cleansers keep the contact time short.

Found inQuick FXSome By Mi
Green tea extract, bentonite clay and calamine

Oil absorbers and mild astringents: clay and calamine soak up sebum, green tea polyphenols help calm and slightly reduce oil.

Found inHada Labo (Rohto)Some By Mi
Protease (enzyme)

An enzyme that loosens the protein “glue” between dead surface cells, a much gentler way to exfoliate than acids; it is in the Round Lab toner.

Found inRound Lab

Retinoids (vitamin A)

Retinol

Vitamin A. It speeds up how fast skin sheds and replaces its surface cells, which keeps pores from clogging, smooths texture and, over months, builds collagen. The first weeks can bring dryness, flaking and redness while skin adjusts, so start with a gentle formula (0.1–0.3%) twice a week; the Philippine Dermatological Society puts results on pimples at 2–4 months. It breaks down in light and leaves skin more sun-sensitive, which makes it a before-sleep product that needs sunscreen on daylight days. Not for use while pregnant or breastfeeding.

Found inLuxe OrganixCeraVeOlayCOSRXFrankly
Retinyl propionate and retinyl palmitate (retinyl esters)

Stored forms of vitamin A that skin turns into retinol and then into its active form. Strength is lost at each step, so they are gentler and slower than retinol; retinyl propionate is the main retinoid in Olay’s Retinol 24.

Found inOlay
Bakuchiol

A plant extract from babchi seeds sold as a gentle retinol alternative. It is not vitamin A and the research on it is still small; in the Luxe Organix cream it is only a trace (100 ppm) beside real retinol.

Found inLuxe Organix

Brighteners and antioxidants

Tranexamic acid (TXA)

Blocks the signal that tells skin to make excess pigment, so post-pimple marks and dark spots fade over months. It does not make skin sun-sensitive.

Found inSKIN1004
Alpha-arbutin

A gentle, well-studied pigment blocker, related to hydroquinone but far milder. Evens tone slowly and is safe at up to 2%.

Found inHada Labo (Rohto)
Vitamin C (ascorbic acid, 3-O-ethyl ascorbic acid, ascorbyl tetraisopalmitate)

An antioxidant that brightens and helps fade the red-brown marks pimples leave. Pure ascorbic acid is the most active form but can tingle; the derivatives in Garnier and Hada Labo are gentler and slower.

Found inHada Labo (Rohto)Quick FXGarnierNaturie (Imju)GarnierCOSRXNiveaMelano CC (Rohto)
Vitamin E (tocopherol, tocopheryl acetate)

An oil-soluble antioxidant that protects skin fats and lips from oxidation and makes other actives, such as vitamin C and sunscreen filters, more stable.

Found inBioré (Kao)Beauty of JoseonHada Labo (Rohto)La Roche-PosayVaselineMedihealSimpleNaturie (Imju)COSRXCetaphilLuxe OrganixMelano CC (Rohto)Vaseline
Licorice (root water, dipotassium glycyrrhizate, glycyrrhetinate)

Calms redness and mildly brightens; one of the few soothing agents that also helps with the marks inflammation leaves behind.

Found inSKIN1004Some By MiCeraVeLuxe OrganixNiveaNaturie (Imju)Melano CC (Rohto)
Rice extract and fermented grains (“probiotics”)

Rich in amino acids and B vitamins; hydrates and soothes. In Relief Sun this is why the sunscreen feels like skincare.

Found inBeauty of Joseon
Royal jelly, honey and xylitol

Sugars and bee-derived extracts used as humectants; honey also has mild antibacterial properties.

Found inBioré (Kao)MedihealHada Labo (Rohto)
Job’s tears (coix seed, hatomugi) extract

A grain extract long used in Japan for smooth, even skin; hydrating and mildly soothing.

Found inNaturie (Imju)Hada Labo (Rohto)Naturie (Imju)
Sea buckthorn fruit extract

An orange berry rich in vitamin C, carotenoids and fatty acids, used as an antioxidant; it is the second ingredient of the Luxe Organix toner.

Found inLuxe Organix
4-Butylresorcinol

A strong brightener from the resorcinol family that blocks the enzyme skin uses to make pigment. It is aimed at dark spots, which you do not have, and is part of why the Nivea toner is sold as “Extra Bright”.

Found inNivea

Soothers

Centella asiatica (cica: asiaticoside, madecassic acid)

A herb with decades of wound-healing research. It reduces redness, supports collagen and helps pimples heal without leaving marks. 72% of the SKIN1004 cream is centella extract.

Found inSKIN1004Hada Labo (Rohto)Some By MiSKIN1004Luxe OrganixFranklyAnuaSmoochkins
Allantoin

A soothing agent that softens rough skin and reduces irritation; often paired with stronger actives.

Found inDermoRepubliqQuick FXRound LabSmoochkins
Aloe leaf extract or juice

Cooling polysaccharides that calm and hydrate; the classic after-sun ingredient.

Found inDermoRepubliqQuick FXSkin Aqua (Sunplay, Rohto)
Scutellaria (Baikal skullcap) root

An antioxidant, anti-redness botanical common in Asian formulas.

Found inHada Labo (Rohto)La Roche-PosayLuxe Organix
Heartleaf (Houttuynia cordata)

A leafy herb (dokdami in Korea, dokudami in Japan) whose water extract calms redness and irritation. It makes up 77% of the Anua toner.

Found inAnua

Sunscreen filters

Modern photostable filters: Tinosorb S, Tinosorb M, Uvinul A Plus (DHHB), Uvinul T150 (ethylhexyl triazone), iscotrizinol

Newer-generation UV filters that cover both UVB (burning) and UVA (ageing and darkening) and do not break down in sunlight. They are why the Korean and Japanese sunscreens feel light yet protect well. Tinosorb M also scatters light the way a mineral filter does.

Found inLa Roche-PosayBeauty of JoseonBioré (Kao)Skin Aqua (Sunplay, Rohto)
Mexoryl 400, Mexoryl XL and Mexoryl SX

L’Oréal’s patented filters. Mexoryl 400 is the only filter on the market that absorbs ultra-long UVA (380–400 nm), the rays that penetrate deepest and reach you through glass.

Found inLa Roche-Posay
Octinoxate (ethylhexyl methoxycinnamate)

An old but effective UVB filter. It loses some strength in sunlight unless stabilized; Bioré pairs it with Tinosorb S, Hikari does not.

Found inBioré (Kao)Skin Aqua (Sunplay, Rohto)
Avobenzone (butyl methoxydibenzoylmethane)

A strong UVA filter that degrades quickly in sunlight unless stabilized, which is the main reason to reapply Hikari when you are outside.

Found inLa Roche-Posay
Titanium dioxide and zinc oxide

Mineral filters that mostly absorb UV rather than reflect it. Very gentle, but they are the cause of white cast. Hikari uses small amounts alongside its chemical filters.

Found inLa Roche-Posay
Ensulizole and bisdisulizole disodium

Water-soluble UVB and UVA filters that let a sunscreen feel like a light gel rather than an oil; they are why Skin Aqua feels so thin.

Found inSkin Aqua (Sunplay, Rohto)

Lip sealers and emollients

Petrolatum (petroleum jelly)

The most effective moisture sealer known; it cuts water loss from skin by more than 98%. It does not add water, it stops the water you already have from escaping, which is exactly what chapped lips need overnight.

Found inVaselineVaseline
Shea butter

A rich plant butter full of fatty acids and anti-inflammatory compounds; softens and seals. Fine on lips, heavier than most oily faces want.

Found inVaselineMedihealCOSRXFrankly
Castor, jojoba, macadamia and olive oils

Plant oils that soften and add slip. Excellent in lip products; on an oily face they are the ingredients to watch if a new serum breaks you out.

Found inVaselineMedihealFranklyVaseline
Lanolin and beeswax

Animal-derived waxes that hold moisture on the lips for hours; lanolin is the closest thing to skin’s own lipids. Avoid if you are allergic to wool or bee products.

Found inVaselineSenka (Shiseido)

Worth knowing

Alcohol (ethanol)

Makes sunscreens and serums feel weightless and dry fast, a plus for oily skin, but it can sting broken or freshly-irritated skin. It is why Bioré and Skin Aqua feel so light.

Found inBioré (Kao)Senka (Shiseido)Skin Aqua (Sunplay, Rohto)GarnierCeraVeNivea
Fragrance (parfum)

Adds scent and nothing else. It is the most common cause of skincare irritation; not a problem for most people, but if your skin ever itches or reddens, the scented products are the first to remove.

Found inNeutrogenaSenka (Shiseido)Bioré (Kao)VaselineMedihealGarnierGarnierLuxe OrganixNiveaMelano CC (Rohto)Vaseline
Dimethicone and other silicones

Give products their smooth, silky glide and form a breathable film that reduces water loss. Non-comedogenic, whatever the internet says.

Found inNeutrogenaHada Labo (Rohto)Bioré (Kao)La Roche-PosayNaturie (Imju)CeraVeOlayCOSRXFranklySmoochkins
Soap-based cleansing agents (fatty acids + potassium hydroxide)

What gives Perfect Whip its dense foam. Cleans oil very well but sits at a higher pH than skin, which is why it can feel tight afterwards.

Found inSenka (Shiseido)Some By Mi
Menthol

Gives lip balms their cooling tingle. Pleasant for many, but it is a mild irritant and can leave already-chapped lips drier, so pick unscented balms for overnight repair.

Found inCarmex

Sources and notes

  • Prices and stock: Watsons Philippines product pages on 26 September 2026 (the retinol step on 27 September, the toner step on 29 September), with the sale prices seen those days noted on the cards. Mercury Drug and St. Joseph Drug were not re-checked this time, so check your branch.
  • Ingredient lists come from the Watsons PH product pages and La Roche-Posay’s own listing; for Klued, Camou and Hikari, which Watsons does not sell, from the SkinSort ingredient database.
  • Photos: product photos come from the retailers’ and brands’ websites, mostly Watsons Philippines; if a photo cannot load, the card shows the brand’s initial instead. Retailer photos are the largest each site publishes (roughly 1,000–1,500 px); none of these products has a 4K retailer image.
  • This is a research summary, not medical advice. If anything stings, burns or breaks you out, stop using it, and see a dermatologist for anything that does not settle in two weeks.

Ang personal care routine mo, nakaayon sa totoong oras ng gising at tulog mo

Para sa oily na balat na paminsan-minsang tinutubuan ng pimple, at para sa night-shift na iskedyul. Na-check ang mga presyo at availability noong 26–29 Setyembre 2026 sa Watsons Philippines at sa Lazada store ng Watsons; madalas itong magbago, kaya gawing gabay lang.

Karaniwang araw mo

Halimbawa lang ang mga oras. Sundin ang katawan mo, hindi ang orasan.

Paano basahin ang routine na ito

  • Routine pag-gising ang unang gagawin mo pagkabangon, kadalasan sa hapon.
  • Routine bago matulog ang huling gagawin mo bago humiga, 2 am man iyon, 4 am, o 9 am.
  • Nakadepende ang sunscreen sa liwanag ng araw, hindi sa oras. Gamitin ito pagkagising kung makakakita ka ng liwanag ng araw bago ka matulog; kasama na ang liwanag mula sa bintana sa desk mo. Laktawan kung gumising ka pagkalubog ng araw at nasa ilalim ka lang ng ilaw hanggang matulog. Kung sumikat ang araw habang nagtatrabaho ka pa malapit sa bintana, mag-apply na noon. Huhugasan mo rin ito bago matulog.
  • Pumili ng isang produkto lang bawat step. Nakalista muna ang kasalukuyang produkto mo; ang mga alternatibo sa ilalim nito ay puwedeng ipalit, hindi idagdag na layer.
  • May niacinamide ang cleanser, serum at retinol cream mo. Sa ganitong dami, ayos lang at karaniwan iyon: mga 5% mula sa serum, bakas lang sa retinol cream, at hinuhugasan naman ang cleanser; wala nito ang moisturizer mo. Mag-ingat lang kung papalit ka sa 10% na serum o magdadagdag ng moisturizer na may niacinamide; itigil kung humapdi o namumula.
  • Isa-isang ipakilala ang bagong produkto at bigyan ng dalawang linggo bago husgahan.
  • Opsyonal para sa iyo ang moisturizer sa araw. Ang cleanser-tapos-sunscreen na routine sa mga video ay tanggap na minimalist na paraan para sa oily na balat: sabi ng mga dermatologist, puwedeng laktawan ang moisturizer sa umaga kung nakakahydrate na ang sunscreen. Ang pinagkakasunduan nila ay mahalaga ang moisturizer bago matulog, at hindi puwedeng laktawan ang sunscreen sa araw. Minarkahan bilang opsyonal ang moisturizer sa routine pag-gising sa ibaba at sinasabi kung kailan ito panatilihin.
  • Mga budget pick. May isa na ring alternatibo sa bawat step na ₱500 pababa kada pinagmulan ng brand (at isang Filipino para sa cleanser). Nakamarka ang mga ito sa card; ang ilan ay mababa sa ₱500 lang sa maliit na laki o sa promo ng Watsons, at nakasaad iyon sa card.
  • Opsyonal ang retinol, at bago matulog lang. Tumutulong itong panatilihing malinis ang pores at makinis ang balat, pero ito ang pinakamalakas na produkto sa planong ito: magsimula sa 2 gabi kada linggo, huwag isabay sa gabing may leave-on na acid o vitamin C na produkto, at ituloy ang sunscreen sa mga paggising na may araw. Itutuloy mo ang Quick FX na may acid sa mga gabing may retinol, kaya bantayan ang paghapdi; ipinapakita ng routine bago matulog kung paano gamitin ang dalawa.
  • Opsyonal ang toner. Matubig na layer ito na idinidiin pagkatapos maghugas, nagdadagdag ng hydration at pampakalmang sangkap bago ang serum; walang mahalagang mawawala kung lalaktawan ito. Nadodoble ng toner na may acid (AHA, BHA o PHA) ang acid ng Quick FX mo: isang beses kada araw lang, at huwag sa mga gabing may retinol.

I-click ang larawan ng produkto para makita ito sa buong laki.

Routine pag-gising

Gawin ito sa loob ng isang oras pagkabangon, anumang oras iyon.

1

Linisin ang mukha

Gaano karami
Kasinlaki ng gisantes hanggang barya. Para sa Perfect Whip, mga 2 cm.
Paano i-apply
Basain ang mukha ng maligamgam na tubig, huwag mainit. Bulain muna sa palad ang gel o foam, saka i-masahe ng buong 60 segundo, dagdagan ang oras sa noo, ilong at baba kung saan nagkukumpol ang langis habang natutulog ka. Banlawan nang mabuti, saka dampian ng malinis na tuwalya hanggang mamasa-masa pa ang balat, hindi tuyong-tuyo.
Saka maghintay
Walang paghihintay. Dumiretso sa toner, o sa serum kung lalaktawan mo ito, habang bahagyang mamasa-masa pa ang balat.
Pumili ng isa sa 8 na ito. Huwag gamitin nang sabay.

2

Toner (opsyonal)

Gaano karami
Mga 1 ml, kasinlaki ng barya sa palad.
Paano i-apply
Pagkatapos maghugas, habang bahagyang mamasa-masa pa ang mukha. Ibuhos sa palad at idiin sa noo, pisngi, ilong at baba gamit ang nakalapat na mga kamay hanggang sumipsip, o basain ang cotton pad at ihaplos palabas mula sa gitna ng mukha nang hindi kinukuskos. Toner ang mga Japanese na “lotion” dito (Hada Labo, Naturie) at ginawa para sa ganitong pagdiin; payo ng Naturie ang ikalawa o ikatlong layer kapag tuyo ang balat. Nadodoble ng toner na may acid (AHA, BHA o PHA) ang acid ng Quick FX mo, kaya isang beses kada araw lang ito gamitin.
Saka maghintay
30–60 segundo, hanggang hindi na basa sa pakiramdam ang balat, saka ang serum.
Laktawan kung
kulang ka sa oras, o humahapdi o parang hilaw ang balat mo; ito ang isang step dito na puwedeng laktawan nang walang mahalagang mawawala. Laktawan ang toner na may acid sa mga gabing may retinol.
Pumili ng isa sa 7 na ito. Huwag gamitin nang sabay.

Sa ₱680 para sa 20 g, ito ang pinakamurang Japanese na serum sa Watsons PH, may 4 na rating na may average na 4.8.

Bantayan. May pabango ito ayon sa listahan ng Rohto, at puwedeng kumiliti ang vitamin C; hindi normal ang paghapdi. Laktawan ito sa mga gabing may retinol, gaya ng sinasabi ng retinol step para sa vitamin C. Mas para ito sa spots at marka kaysa sa langis, at 4 na rating lang ang mayroon sa Watsons. Ang kahon kasama ang tube ang nasa larawan.

4

Mag-moisturizer (opsyonal para sa oily na balat)

Gaano karami
Kasinlaki ng gisantes hanggang blueberry.
Paano i-apply
Painitin muna sa pagitan ng dalawang daliri, idiin muna sa pisngi at ihaplos palabas at pataas, saka tapusin ang noo, ilong at baba gamit ang natira. Manipis at pantay na layer ang kailangan ng oily na balat, hindi makapal.
Saka maghintay
2–3 minuto bago mag-sunscreen, para pantay ang pagkalatag ng sunscreen sa ibabaw.
Laktawan kung
nakakahydrate na ang sunscreen mo at hindi masikip ang balat pagkahugas. Sang-ayon ang mga dermatologist na puwedeng cleanser → serum → sunscreen lang ang oily na balat sa araw; ang moisturizer ang naging pinakamahalaga bago matulog. Panatilihin ito kung walang sunscreen sa paggising, o kung masikip o nagbabalat ang balat mo.
Pumili ng isa sa 8 na ito. Huwag gamitin nang sabay.

5

Sunscreen, kung makakakita ka lang ng liwanag ng araw

Gaano karami
Dalawang buong haba ng daliri, mga isang-kapat na kutsarita, para sa mukha at leeg.
Paano i-apply
Tuldukan ang noo, magkabilang pisngi, ilong at baba, saka ikalat gamit ang nakalapat na mga daliri hanggang pumantay. Ilatag ito sa halip na kuskusin na parang lotion; nagpapanipis ng proteksiyon ang pagkuskos. Isama ang tenga at batok kung lalabas ka.
Saka maghintay
10–15 minuto bago lumabas o umupo sa direktang liwanag mula sa bintana. Mag-reapply tuwing 2 oras kung nasa direktang araw o pagkatapos pawisan; kung nasa loob lang malapit sa bintana, sapat ang isang apply bawat gising.
Laktawan kung
wala ka talagang makikitang liwanag ng araw bago matulog.
Pumili ng isa sa 8 na ito. Huwag gamitin nang sabay.

6

Gaano karami
Isang manipis at pantay na hagod; kaunti pang dagdag sa mga gilid.
Paano i-apply
I-apply pagkatapos ng sunscreen, bilang huling step. Halos walang melanin na proteksyon ang labi, kaya kung makakakita ka ng araw, pumili ng may SPF: ang Luxe Organix Lipscreen mo (SPF50+) at ang Beach Hut (SPF100) ang umaabot sa SPF 30 na hinihingi ng American Academy of Dermatology sa labas, samantalang SPF15 ang Nivea Med Repair, Carmex, Some By Mi at Mentholatum, kaya mag-reapply nang madalas. Kung walang araw sa paggising, sapat na ang manipis na layer ng anumang balm mula sa night step (Vaseline, Nivea Original Care, Dr.Jart, Mediheal). Mag-reapply pagkatapos kumain o uminom.
Saka maghintay
Wala nang kasunod. Noong 29 Setyembre 2026, iisa lang ang Korean at iisa lang ang Japanese na lip balm na may SPF sa Watsons PH (parehong SPF15 at nasa step na ito), kaya walang laman ang ibang Korean at Japanese na slot.
Pumili ng isa sa 6 na ito. Huwag gamitin nang sabay.

Routine bago matulog

Gawin ito bago ka talaga humiga, 2 am man iyon, 4 am, o 9 am. Ang layunin ay tanggalin ang sunscreen, pawis at langis ng buong araw bago mo ito matulugan. Kung nakatulog ka sa laptop, maglinis agad pagkagising.

1

Linisin ang mukha

Iyon ding produktong pinili mo sa routine pag-gising. Sapat na ang isang mabining paghuhugas para matanggal ang mga gel na sunscreen na ito. Kung marami kang ginamit na sunscreen at may natitira pang film, maghugas ulit gamit ang parehong produkto sa halip na bumili ng hiwalay na oil cleanser.

2

Toner (opsyonal)

Iyon ding produktong pinili mo sa routine pag-gising. Ganoon ding dami, idinidiin pagkatapos maghugas. Sa mga gabing may retinol, gamitin lang kung walang acid, o laktawan ito, para walang madagdag sa acid ng Quick FX sa ilalim ng retinol.

3

Serum

Iyon ding produktong pinili mo sa routine pag-gising. 2–3 patak din, idiin sa mamasa-masang balat. Maghintay ng 30–60 segundo.

4

Retinol, dalawang beses kada linggo sa umpisa (opsyonal)

Gaano karami
Isang kasinlaki ng gisantes para sa buong mukha; hindi bibilis ang epekto kapag dinamihan, iirita lang ang balat.
Paano i-apply
Sa mga gabing may retinol, iwasan ang leave-on na acid at vitamin C. Quick FX pa rin ang panghugas mo, pero may acid ito, at mas mataas ang tsansa ng paghapdi at pagbabalat kapag magkasabay ang acid at retinol sa iisang gabi: bulain lang ito ng 20–30 segundo, banlawan nang mabuti, saka hayaang matuyo nang lubusan ang mukha bago mag-retinol (payo ng American Academy of Dermatology na maghintay ng 20–30 minuto pagkahugas); mas humahapdi ang basang balat. Puwedeng ilagay sa pagitan ang niacinamide serum. Tuldukan ng retinol ang noo, pisngi at baba at ikalat nang manipis, iwas sa mata, sa gilid ng ilong at sa labi. Gamitin ito 2 gabi kada linggo sa unang buwan, saka bawat ikalawang gabi kung kalmado pa rin ang balat.
Saka maghintay
Isara agad ang takip, saka mag-moisturizer pagkalipas ng ilang minuto. Normal ang kaunting pagkatuyo, pagbabalat o pamumula sa unang mga linggo habang nag-a-adjust ang balat; kung humahapdi o nagbabalat, bawasan ang dalas, o mag-moisturizer muna bago ang retinol. Husgahan ito pagkalipas ng 2–4 na buwan para sa pimples; mas matagal para sa fine lines. Nasisira ang retinol sa liwanag, kaya kung natutulog ka habang may araw, isara ang kurtina, at mag-sunscreen sa bawat paggising na may araw.
Laktawan kung
nasunog sa araw, nagbabalat, humahapdi o bagong iritado ang balat mo; nagpa-peel ka o gumamit ng leave-on na produktong may AHA o BHA noong gabing iyon (ang hinuhugasang Quick FX mo ang eksepsiyong pinili mo); o buntis ka, nagpapasuso o sinusubukang magbuntis.
Pumili ng isa sa 6 na ito. Huwag gamitin nang sabay.

5

Mag-moisturizer

Iyon ding produktong pinili mo sa routine pag-gising. Bago matulog, puwedeng bahagyang dagdagan, kasinlaki ng blueberry, dahil wala nang ipapatong. Kung malagkit ka paggising, bumalik sa kasinlaki ng gisantes.

6

Lip treatment, ang pinakahuli bago matulog

Gaano karami
Makapal na layer, higit sa isusuot mo sa araw.
Paano i-apply
Maghintay ng isang minuto pagkatapos ng moisturizer para hindi ito dumudulas, saka i-apply gamit ang malinis na dulo ng daliri, o ang spatula para sa tub, mula gitna ng labi palabas. Huwag kuskusin at huwag dilaan; matulog na. Punasan ng tissue ang natira paggising. Kung nagbabalat ang labi, sapat na ang mabining pagkuskos ng basang malambot na tuwalya isang beses kada linggo; laktawan ang sugar scrub.
Saka maghintay
Wala nang ipapatong pagkatapos nito. Matulog na.
Pumili ng isa sa 7 na ito. Huwag gamitin nang sabay.

Bakit bagay sa iyo. Ang pinaka-nirebyu na lip balm na walang tint sa Watsons PH (397 rating, 364 nakasulat na review): shea butter at castor oil, parehong nasa listahan ng American Academy of Dermatology para sa tuyong labi, sa plain na stick na walang kulay. ₱162, at ₱81 sa kalahating presyo nang i-check.

Bakit bagay sa iyo. Ang plain na lip treatment mula sa Japanese brand na may pinakamaraming rating sa Watsons PH nang i-check: petrolatum gel, ang occlusive na inirerekomenda ng American Academy of Dermatology para sa tuyong labi, sa ₱135 para sa 8 g, may 13 rating sa Watsons.

Ang menthol, camphor at eucalyptus ang nagbibigay ng malamig na kiliti, at nasa listahan ng American Academy of Dermatology ang tatlo ng mga sangkap na iiwasan sa bitak-bitak na labi, kaya sa mga gabing nagbibitak ang labi, mas banayad ang plain na petroleum jelly. SPF 15 ang nakasulat sa larawan ng pakete, pero walang sunscreen filter sa ingredient list na inilathala ng Watsons, kaya huwag itong asahan sa araw. Gawa sa China.

Kung may tumubong pimple

Huwag pisilin. Pagkatapos ng moisturizer, ang hydrocolloid pimple patch (halimbawa, Acnes patch, ₱230 sa Watsons) ang nag-iisang dagdag na ligtas isama. Tanggalin paggising.

Bawat aktibong sangkap, ipinaliwanag

Lahat ng aktibo at kapansin-pansing sangkap sa 57 produkto sa itaas, sa simpleng salita, kasama ang mga produktong naglalaman ng bawat isa.

Pampahydrate at pampatibay ng skin barrier

Hyaluronic acid (sodium hyaluronate)

Isang molekulang asukal na humahawak ng tubig na ilang ulit sa sarili nitong timbang. Hinihila nito ang moisture papunta sa itaas na layer ng balat para maging malusog sa halip na masikip ang pakiramdam. Pinakaepektibo kung ipapahid sa mamasa-masang balat at sasaraduhan ng moisturizer.

Para sa langis, pores at pimples

Ang pinakamasipag na sangkap ng routine mo. Nagre-regulate ito ng produksyon ng langis, nagpapakalma ng pamumula, nagpapatibay ng barrier at unti-unting nagpapantay ng kulay. Sapat na ang 2–5%; kaunti lang ang dagdag ng 10% para sa karamihan at mas malaki ang tsansa ng paghapdi.

Mga retinoid (vitamin A)

Retinol

Vitamin A. Pinabibilis nito ang pagpapalit ng balat sa mga cell sa ibabaw, kaya hindi nababarahan ang pores, kuminis ang texture at, sa loob ng ilang buwan, dumarami ang collagen. Sa unang mga linggo, puwedeng matuyo, magbalat at mamula ang balat habang nag-a-adjust, kaya magsimula sa mabining formula (0.1–0.3%) dalawang beses kada linggo; ayon sa Philippine Dermatological Society, 2–4 na buwan bago makita ang epekto sa pimples. Nasisira ito sa liwanag at nagpapa-sensitive sa araw, kaya pang-bago matulog ito at kailangan ng sunscreen sa mga araw na may araw. Hindi para sa buntis o nagpapasuso.

NasaLuxe OrganixCeraVeOlayCOSRXFrankly
Retinyl propionate at retinyl palmitate (mga retinyl ester)

Mga nakaimbak na anyo ng vitamin A na ginagawang retinol ng balat at saka ang aktibong anyo nito. Humihina ito sa bawat hakbang, kaya mas mabini at mas mabagal kaysa retinol; retinyl propionate ang pangunahing retinoid sa Olay Retinol 24.

NasaOlay
Bakuchiol

Extract mula sa buto ng halamang babchi na ibinebentang mabining alternatibo sa retinol. Hindi ito vitamin A at kaunti pa ang pananaliksik dito; bakas lang ito (100 ppm) sa Luxe Organix cream, katabi ng totoong retinol.

NasaLuxe Organix

Mga sunscreen filter

Mga modernong photostable filter: Tinosorb S, Tinosorb M, Uvinul A Plus (DHHB), Uvinul T150 (ethylhexyl triazone), iscotrizinol

Mga bagong henerasyong UV filter na sumasaklaw sa UVB (pagkasunog) at UVA (pagtanda at pangingitim) at hindi nasisira sa araw. Ito ang dahilan kung bakit magaan pero mahusay ang proteksyon ng mga Korean at Japanese na sunscreen. Nagkakalat din ng liwanag ang Tinosorb M tulad ng mineral filter.

Mga patented na filter ng L’Oréal. Ang Mexoryl 400 ang tanging filter sa merkado na sumisipsip ng ultra-long UVA (380–400 nm), ang mga sinag na pinakamalalim tumagos at umaabot sa iyo sa pamamagitan ng salamin.

NasaLa Roche-Posay

Mineral na filter na kadalasang sumisipsip ng UV sa halip na nagre-reflect. Napakabini, pero ito ang sanhi ng white cast. Gumagamit ang Hikari ng kaunti kasama ng mga chemical filter nito.

Mga pinagkunan at paalala

  • Presyo at stock: mga product page ng Watsons Philippines noong 26 Setyembre 2026 (ang retinol step noong 27 Setyembre, ang toner step noong 29 Setyembre), at nakatala sa mga card ang mga sale price na nakita sa mga araw na iyon. Hindi na-check muli ang Mercury Drug at St. Joseph Drug ngayon, kaya i-check ang branch mo.
  • Ang mga listahan ng sangkap ay mula sa mga product page ng Watsons PH at sa sariling listing ng La Roche-Posay; para sa Klued, Camou at Hikari, na hindi ibinebenta sa Watsons, mula sa ingredient database ng SkinSort.
  • Mga larawan: galing sa mga website ng retailer at ng brand ang mga larawan ng produkto, karamihan ay mula sa Watsons Philippines; kung hindi ma-load ang isang larawan, unang titik ng brand ang lalabas sa card. Ang mga larawan ng retailer ang pinakamalaking inilathala ng bawat site (mga 1,000–1,500 px); walang 4K na larawan mula sa retailer para sa mga produktong ito.
  • Buod ito ng pananaliksik, hindi medikal na payo. Kung may humapdi, sumunog o nagpa-breakout, itigil ang paggamit, at magpatingin sa dermatologist kung hindi humupa sa loob ng dalawang linggo.

Top picks: one product per step

Eight products chosen from the 57 in this plan. Each is the one most people in the Philippines actually buy and rate well, suits oily, occasionally pimple-prone skin, and can be picked up at Watsons. The alternatives on the routine page are there for when a top pick is out of stock or disagrees with your skin.

How these were chosen, and how sure I am

“Most popular” here means what Filipinos actually buy and review at Watsons PH as of 29 September 2026 (review counts checked product by product), backed by press and retailer best-seller lists. There is no public product-level “best of 2026” list for the Philippines: the Watsons HWB Awards 2026 (15 May 2026) honoured brands and distributors, not individual products, so every pick below shows its own evidence. Where the most-reviewed product is not the pick, the card says so and explains why. Popularity was then weighed against fit for oily, occasionally pimple-prone skin, price, and walk-in availability.

Your top-pick routine at a glance

Wake-up: Quick FX (or Cetaphil Gentle Foaming if your skin feels tight) → Luxe Organix Ultralight Glow Toner (optional) → DermoRepubliq → Celeteque Hydration (optional) → Bioré if you will see daylight → Luxe Organix Lipscreen.

Before sleep: Quick FX (Cetaphil Gentle Foaming on retinol nights) → Luxe Organix Ultralight Glow Toner (optional) → DermoRepubliq → Luxe Organix Retinol + Bakuchiol, twice a week to start → Celeteque Hydration → Vaseline.

Starter cost for all eight: about ₱2,506 at the main sizes; together they last roughly two months, with the serum stretching to three or four and the retinol to about seven.

1
Top pick Cleanser
Your current product₱500 and under
Quick FX Pimple Eraser Facial Gel Cleanser
Sizes, prices and how long each lasts
SizePriceLasts about
150 ml₱329about 2½ months
75 ml₱199about 5 weeks
Where to buy
Watsons (in store and online); Quick FX official Shopee and Lazada stores
Where it fits
Cleanser: Step 1 of both routines
Key actives
Salicylic acid (BHA)Glycolic acid (AHA)NiacinamideTea tree oilAloeAllantoinEthyl ascorbic acid (vitamin C)

Why it fits you. Your wash for both routines, from a Filipino brand: salicylic acid (BHA) keeps pores clear for the odd pimple, a little glycolic acid (AHA) smooths, and niacinamide, tea tree and aloe calm, in a sulfate-free gel that rinses clean on oily skin. The 75 ml has 191 Watsons ratings; the 150 ml is ₱329.

Watch out. Two exfoliating acids, twice a day, and you keep it on retinol nights too. Warning: acid and retinol on the same night raise the risk of stinging, redness and peeling. On those nights, lather it for 20–30 seconds instead of 60, rinse well and let your face dry completely before the retinol; if your skin stings or peels, skip the retinol that night. Its label warns that the glycolic acid (an AHA) raises sun sensitivity, so wear sunscreen on every daylight wake-up. It is fragrance-free, though tea tree oil has a scent of its own.

Why this one over the others. Kept for fit over the more-rated washes: the Luxe Organix Acne Derm+ gel at the top of the Watsons ratings foams with sodium laureth sulfate and has no pimple-fighting acid, while Quick FX uses a sulfate-free betaine base with salicylic and glycolic acids, niacinamide and tea tree for oily, pimple-prone skin. The 150 ml is ₱329 and the 75 ml ₱199, both under ₱500. It is an acid wash, so on retinol nights the runner-up is the wash to use.
Evidence (29 September 2026). Transparency: the most-rated face washes in Watsons PH’s best-seller list are Luxe Organix Acne Derm+ Gel Cleanser (549 ratings, ₱289), Garnier Anti-Acne 3-in-1 50 ml (524, ₱150) and Celeteque Hydration Facial Wash 100 ml (243, ₱185). The Luxe is SLES-based with tea tree as its only anti-pimple ingredient and the Garnier is a soap foam with pumice and fragrance, so Quick FX (191 ratings on the 75 ml, 14 on the 150 ml, both in stock) is recommended for fit, not popularity. Cetaphil Gentle Foaming: 229 ratings, 2,000+ sold, ₱604, in stock (checked 29 Sep 2026).

Mga top pick: isang produkto kada step

Walong produkto na pinili mula sa 57 sa planong ito. Bawat isa ay ang pinakabinibili at mataas ang rating sa Pilipinas, bagay sa oily at paminsan-minsang pimple-prone na balat, at mabibili sa Watsons. Nandoon ang mga alternatibo sa routine page kung out of stock ang top pick o hindi bagay sa balat mo.

Paano pinili ang mga ito, at gaano ako sigurado

Ang “pinakasikat” dito ay kung ano talaga ang binibili at nirerebyu ng mga Pilipino sa Watsons PH noong 29 Setyembre 2026 (na-check ang bilang ng review kada produkto), na sinusuportahan ng press at mga best-seller list ng retailer. Walang pampublikong “best of 2026” na listahan kada produkto para sa Pilipinas: ang Watsons HWB Awards 2026 (15 Mayo 2026) ay nagparangal sa mga brand at distributor, hindi sa indibidwal na produkto, kaya may sariling ebidensya ang bawat pick sa ibaba. Kung hindi ang pinaka-nirebyu ang pick, sinasabi iyon ng card at ipinapaliwanag kung bakit. Pagkatapos, tinimbang ang popularidad laban sa pagkabagay sa oily at paminsan-minsang pimple-prone na balat, presyo, at availability sa tindahan.

Ang top-pick routine mo sa isang tingin

Pag-gising: Quick FX (o Cetaphil Gentle Foaming kung masikip ang balat) → Luxe Organix Ultralight Glow Toner (opsyonal) → DermoRepubliq → Celeteque Hydration (opsyonal) → Bioré kung makakakita ng araw → Luxe Organix Lipscreen.

Bago matulog: Quick FX (Cetaphil Gentle Foaming sa mga gabing may retinol) → Luxe Organix Ultralight Glow Toner (opsyonal) → DermoRepubliq → Luxe Organix Retinol + Bakuchiol, dalawang beses kada linggo sa umpisa → Celeteque Hydration → Vaseline.

Panimulang gastos para sa walo: about ₱2,506 sa pangunahing laki; magkasama, mga dalawang buwan ang tagal, at umaabot sa tatlo hanggang apat ang serum at mga pito ang retinol.

Bantayan. Dalawang exfoliating acid, dalawang beses kada araw, at ginagamit mo rin ito sa mga gabing may retinol. Babala: mas mataas ang tsansa ng paghapdi, pamumula at pagbabalat kapag magkasabay ang acid at retinol sa iisang gabi. Sa mga gabing iyon, bulain lang ito ng 20–30 segundo sa halip na 60, banlawan nang mabuti at hayaang matuyo nang lubusan ang mukha bago ang retinol; kung humapdi o magbalat ang balat, laktawan ang retinol sa gabing iyon. Nakasaad sa label na nagpapa-sensitive sa araw ang glycolic acid (isang AHA), kaya mag-sunscreen sa bawat paggising na may araw. Walang pabango ito, bagama’t may sariling amoy ang tea tree oil.

Bakit ito at hindi ang iba. Pinanatili dahil mas bagay kaysa sa mga wash na mas marami ang rating: sodium laureth sulfate ang pambula ng Luxe Organix Acne Derm+ gel na nangunguna sa rating sa Watsons at wala itong acid na panlaban sa pimple, samantalang sulfate-free na betaine base ang gamit ng Quick FX, na may salicylic at glycolic acid, niacinamide at tea tree para sa oily at pimple-prone na balat. ₱329 ang 150 ml at ₱199 ang 75 ml, parehong mababa sa ₱500. Acid wash ito, kaya sa mga gabing may retinol, ang pangalawa ang panghugas.

SLES ang base ng Luxe at tea tree lang ang panlaban nito sa pimple, at soap foam na may pumice at pabango ang Garnier, kaya inirerekomenda ang Quick FX (191 rating sa 75 ml, 14 sa 150 ml, parehong may stock) dahil sa pagkabagay, hindi sa kasikatan. Why it fits you. Your wash for both routines, from a Filipino brand: salicylic acid (BHA) keeps pores clear for the odd pimple, a little glycolic acid (AHA) smooths, and niacinamide, tea tree and aloe calm, in a sulfate-free gel that rinses clean on oily skin. On those nights, lather it for 20–30 seconds instead of 60, rinse well and let your face dry completely before the retinol; if your skin stings or peels, skip the retinol that night. Its label warns that the glycolic acid (an AHA) raises sun sensitivity, so wear sunscreen on every daylight wake-up. It is fragrance-free, though tea tree oil has a scent of its own.

Watch out. Watsons PH shows no ratings for it yet, so its place here rests on the best-seller list, not on local reviews. ₱1,400 for 250 ml (about 4 months) is steep; the 40 ml at ₱400 lasts about 3 weeks, a trial more than a buy. The Watsons photo shows a 150 ml bottle; the listing is the 250 ml.

Lahat ng 57 produkto

Lahat ng produkto sa planong ito sa isang lugar. I-filter ayon sa uri, pinagmulan ng brand o budget, ayusin ayon sa presyo, at i-click ang larawan para makita sa buong laki. Ayon sa unang nakalistang laki ang pag-aayos ng presyo ng mga produktong maraming laki.

Quick FX Pimple Eraser Facial Gel Cleanser
Laki, presyo at gaano katagal ang bawat isa
LakiPresyoTatagal ng mga
150 ml₱329mga 2½ buwan
75 ml₱199mga 5 linggo
Saan mabibili
Watsons (tindahan at online); official Shopee at Lazada store ng Quick FX
Saan ito ginagamit
Cleanser: Step 1 ng parehong routine
Pangunahing aktibong sangkap
Salicylic acid (BHA)Glycolic acid (AHA)NiacinamideTea tree oilAloeAllantoinEthyl ascorbic acid (vitamin C)

Bakit bagay sa iyo. Ang panghugas mo sa parehong routine, mula sa Filipino brand: pinananatiling malinis ng salicylic acid (BHA) ang pores para sa paminsan-minsang pimple, nagpapakinis ang kaunting glycolic acid (AHA), at pampakalma ang niacinamide, tea tree at aloe, sa sulfate-free na gel na malinis ang banlaw sa oily na balat. 191 ang rating ng 75 ml sa Watsons; ₱329 ang 150 ml.

Bantayan. Dalawang exfoliating acid, dalawang beses kada araw, at ginagamit mo rin ito sa mga gabing may retinol. Babala: mas mataas ang tsansa ng paghapdi, pamumula at pagbabalat kapag magkasabay ang acid at retinol sa iisang gabi. Sa mga gabing iyon, bulain lang ito ng 20–30 segundo sa halip na 60, banlawan nang mabuti at hayaang matuyo nang lubusan ang mukha bago ang retinol; kung humapdi o magbalat ang balat, laktawan ang retinol sa gabing iyon. Nakasaad sa label na nagpapa-sensitive sa araw ang glycolic acid (isang AHA), kaya mag-sunscreen sa bawat paggising na may araw. Walang pabango ito, bagama’t may sariling amoy ang tea tree oil.
